# SEO Sweep — 2026-10-27

Audit type: **weekly link & crawl sweep** (CADENCE.md §3.3). Evaluated against the built `dist/` on production `main` @ `8e33aea`. `npm run build` ✅ (87 pages: 58 posts, 26 tag pages, homepage, 2 author/about pages). **1999** (rule, URL) evaluations across all 41 SEO-PLAYBOOK rules; **0 unevaluable**. No future-dated posts remain (all scheduled posts have published), so there is no IL-03 forward-reference carve-out this cycle.

> Built on Node 22 (environment did not provide the `.nvmrc` Node 24); Astro 5 emits the same `dist/` output, so the audit is unaffected.

## Disposition — no new fixes land this cycle (idempotent, CADENCE §0.5)

Every mechanical violation found is **already pending in an open draft PR**, so re-fixing here would create a competing duplicate of in-flight work.

### MT-02 (blocker) — 8 over-length meta descriptions

`<meta name="description">` must be 70–160 chars. All 8 failing posts are **already trimmed in open draft PRs** and clear on human merge of those PRs — not duplicated here:

| Post | chars | Pending in |
|------|-------|-----------|
| `bring-your-own-broker` | 206 | #86 |
| `can-waev-write-to-your-broker` | 181 | #86 |
| `keeping-control-of-your-mesh-network` | 174 | #86 |
| `reading-the-signal` | 176 | #86 |
| `setting-up-mosquitto-for-meshcore` | 209 | #86 |
| `the-map-you-didnt-draw` | 252 | #87 |
| `what-we-choose-not-to-know` | 180 | #86 |
| `your-off-grid-mesh-buildout` | 171 | #86 |

PR **#86** trims 7 descriptions; PR **#87** trims `the-map-you-didnt-draw` (252→149). Together they cover all 8. Both remain **unmerged drafts** — a human merge of both clears every MT-02 blocker. (MT-02 over-length trimming is this role's established in-PR fix — merged PR #80, open PRs #86/#87.)

### IL-02 (major) — 7 posts with < 2 in-prose cross-links

Non-mechanical: the auditor never edits post prose (brief Step 3). Tracked continuously in **issue #85**. This run's failing set is **identical** to the set recorded in #85 (narrowed to 7 on 2026-09-29) — a continuity comment is posted for the 2026-10-27 cycle; the issue body is not re-edited.

### HP-04 (minor) — one heading-order skip

`/blog/the-opinionated-mesh-analyzer/` renders value-card `<h3>`s above the first body `<h2>` (component-driven layout, not authored prose). Minor; reported only, no fix this cycle.

## Link & crawl health — all green

- **IL-03** (no broken internal links): PASS on all 87 pages. No future-dated posts remain, so there is no scheduled-post forward-reference carve-out this cycle — every internal `/…` href resolves to a built path.
- **IL-04** (no orphan posts): PASS — every one of the 58 published posts has in-degree ≥ 1.
- **MT-03** (self-canonical): PASS on all 87 pages. **SM-02**: all sitemap URLs match their page canonicals.
- **SD-11** (JSON-LD valid, no placeholders): PASS on all 87 pages. **SD-01/02/03/04/05/06/07/08/09/10** all PASS.
- **SM-01** sitemap indexes all 85 public URLs (home + 58 posts + 26 tags), excludes drafts/future. **SM-03** llms.txt lists exactly the 58 published posts. **SM-04** canon intact. **SM-05** RSS valid. **SM-06** no robots.txt (acceptable).
- **TG-01–TG-05**: all tag pages built, membership exact, CollectionPage schema present, badges linked, indexable.
- **MT-01/04/05/06/07/08/09/10**, **HP-01/02/03**: all PASS.

**One item for a human eyeball — not a defect, no fix made:** `letsmesh.net/` (linked from `four-tools-one-mesh.mdx`) returned **HTTP 403** to the sweep's honest user-agent — the server answered with a bot/WAF challenge rather than a timeout or NXDOMAIN, so the destination is almost certainly live for ordinary visitors. Removing or rewriting an outbound link on the strength of a WAF 403 is an editorial judgment the auditor does not make mechanically. Bare `fonts.googleapis.com`/`fonts.gstatic.com` origins return 404 but are `<link rel="preconnect">` hints, not navigable links; the resolved font-CSS URL returns 200.

- **Issue #85** (IL-02, 7 posts) — editorial prose work; continuity comment posted for this cycle, set unchanged since 2026-09-29.
- **MT-07**: no action — omission is the ratified passing state (SEO-PLAYBOOK §2, 2026-07-04). **SD-06** passes (no source-repo `sameAs`), **SM-04** canon intact — **no `human-approval` findings this run.**
